# IMPERIA COLD WAR — French political leaders and remembered party choices.
COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_georges_pompidou = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Georges Pompidou"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = { portrait = GFX_COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_georges_pompidou_portrait }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_0_pierre_debizet = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Pierre Debizet"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 0 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Pierre Debizet"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/pierre_debizet.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_pierre_debizet_desc
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 0 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_pierre_debizet = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_choose_0_pierre_debizet = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_0 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_0_pierre_debizet = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1970.01.01
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_0_pierre_debizet = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_0_pierre_debizet = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1970.01.01
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Pierre Debizet"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jacques Foccart"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_jacques_foccart = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_pierre_debizet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_0_jacques_foccart = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_0 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_0
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_0 = yes
	}
}

				name = "Georges Pompidou"

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1974.12.14
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chirac = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_1_jacques_chirac = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1974.4.2
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chaban_delmas = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_1_jacques_chaban_delmas = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_1_georges_pompidou = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1974.4.2
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chaban_delmas = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1974.12.14
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chirac = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Georges Pompidou"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jacques Chaban-Delmas"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Pierre Messmer"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jacques Chirac"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Robert Poujade"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 5 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "René Tomasini"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 6 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Alain Peyrefitte"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 7 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Alexandre Sanguinetti"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 8 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "André Bord"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 9 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Yves Guéna"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Michel Debré"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 11 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chaban_delmas = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_pierre_messmer = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chirac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_robert_poujade = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_rene_tomasini = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 6 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_alain_peyrefitte = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 7 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_alexandre_sanguinetti = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 8 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_andre_bord = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 9 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_yves_guena = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 10 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_michel_debre = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 11 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_georges_pompidou = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_pierre_messmer = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_jacques_chirac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_robert_poujade = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_rene_tomasini = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 6 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_alain_peyrefitte = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 7 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_1_alexandre_sanguinetti = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 8 }

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes

			western_liberals_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Valéry Giscard d'Estaing"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Michel Poniatowski"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jean-Pierre Soisson"
					ruling_only = yes
				}

					name = "Raymond Barre"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}

			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }

					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_2_michel_poniatowski = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_2_jean_pierre_soisson = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_2_francois_leotard = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_2_gerard_longuet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 6 }

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_liberals_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_2
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_2 = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_3_alain_savary = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_3_alain_savary = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Alain Savary"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 3 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Alain Savary"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/alain_savary.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_alain_savary_desc
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 3 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_alain_savary = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_choose_3_alain_savary = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_3_alain_savary = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_3 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_3_alain_savary = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1971.6.16
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_3_francois_mitterrand = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_3_francois_mitterrand = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_3_alain_savary = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1971.6.16
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_3_francois_mitterrand = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Alain Savary"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "François Mitterrand"

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			western_social_democrats_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_3
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_3 = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_4_waldeck_rochet = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Waldeck Rochet"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 4 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Waldeck Rochet"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/waldeck_rochet.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_waldeck_rochet_desc
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 4 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_waldeck_rochet = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_choose_4_waldeck_rochet = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_4 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_4 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1972.12.17
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_georges_marchais = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_4_georges_marchais = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1970.01.01
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
	}
}

				}
			}

		}

					name = "Georges Marchais"
					ruling_only = yes
				}

					name = "Jacques Duclos"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_georges_marchais = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_jacques_duclos = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_jacques_duclos = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_georges_marchais = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_4_waldeck_rochet = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_5_alain_krivine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_5_alain_krivine = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Alain Krivine"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 5 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Alain Krivine"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/alain_krivine.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_alain_krivine_desc
				ideology = anarchist_communism
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 5 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_alain_krivine = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_13_simon_nora = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_13_simon_nora = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Simon Nora"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 13 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Simon Nora"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/simon_nora.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_simon_nora_desc
				ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 13 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_simon_nora = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_13 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_autocracy_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Simon Nora"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Raymond Barre"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_13_raymond_barre = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_13_simon_nora = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_13_simon_nora = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_13_raymond_barre = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_13 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_14_alain_poher = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_14_jacques_duhamel = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_14 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_14 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_14
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_14 = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_paul_huvelin = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Paul Huvelin"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 15 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Paul Huvelin"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/paul_huvelin.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_paul_huvelin_desc
				ideology = oligarchism
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 15 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_paul_huvelin = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_choose_15_paul_huvelin = {

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_15 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1987.1.1
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_francois_perigot = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1982.1.1
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1973.1.1
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1970.01.01
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_paul_huvelin = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_15_paul_huvelin = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_15 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_oligarch_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1970.01.01
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1973.1.1
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1982.1.1
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1987.1.1
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Paul Huvelin"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "François Ceyrac"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Yvon Gattaz"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "François Perigot"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_paul_huvelin = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_ceyrac = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_yvon_gattaz = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_15_francois_perigot = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }

		if = {
			limit = {

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_17 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_green_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Pierre Aguesse"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "René Dumont"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Brice Lalonde"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Solange Fernex"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Antoine Waechter"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 5 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_rene_dumont = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_brice_lalonde = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_solange_fernex = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_antoine_waechter = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_pierre_aguesse = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_brice_lalonde = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_solange_fernex = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_antoine_waechter = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_pierre_aguesse = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_rene_dumont = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_solange_fernex = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_antoine_waechter = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_pierre_aguesse = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_rene_dumont = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_brice_lalonde = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_antoine_waechter = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_pierre_aguesse = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_rene_dumont = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_brice_lalonde = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_solange_fernex = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_pierre_aguesse = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_rene_dumont = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_brice_lalonde = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_solange_fernex = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_17_antoine_waechter = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_17 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_19 = {
	COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_19_benny_levy = yes
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_19 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_communism_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Benny Lévy"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Alain Geismar"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_19_alain_geismar = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_19_benny_levy = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_19_benny_levy = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_19_alain_geismar = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_19 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_19 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			neutrality_neutral_communism_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_19
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_19 = yes
	}
}

			NOT = {
				date < 1972.10.5
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_marie_le_pen = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_20_jean_marie_le_pen = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_20_gerard_nicoud = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_20 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			nationalist_right_wing_populists_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1972.10.5
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_marie_le_pen = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Gérard Nicoud"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jean-Marie Le Pen"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jean-Pierre Stirbois"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Bruno Mégret"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "François Duprat"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 5 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }

				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }

				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_pierre_stirbois = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_bruno_megret = yes
				}

			}

				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}

				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_gerard_nicoud = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_marie_le_pen = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_pierre_stirbois = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_bruno_megret = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_gerard_nicoud = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_marie_le_pen = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_jean_pierre_stirbois = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_bruno_megret = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_20_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 5 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_20 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_20 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			nationalist_right_wing_populists_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_20
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_20 = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_21_jean_francois_galvaire = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							name = "Jean-François Galvaire"
							ruling_only = yes
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed }
					NOT = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 21 }
					}
				}
			}
			retire_country_leader = yes
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Jean-François Galvaire"
				picture = "gfx/leaders/FRA/COLDWAR_1970/jean_francois_galvaire.dds"
				desc = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_jean_francois_galvaire_desc
				ideology = Nat_Fascism
				traits = {

				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_appointed
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_active_slot = 21 }
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_portrait_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_initialized } COLDWAR_FRA_update_party_programmes = yes }
		update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_choose_21_jean_francois_galvaire = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_21 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_21 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				date < 1970.5.28
			}
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_alain_robert = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_21_alain_robert = yes
	}
	else = {
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_apply_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_21 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			nationalist_fascist_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					date < 1970.5.28
				}
				COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_alain_robert = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Jean-François Galvaire"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Alain Robert"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "François Duprat"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_alain_robert = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_alain_robert = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_jean_francois_galvaire = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_alain_robert = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_21_francois_duprat = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 3 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_21 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_23 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_country = yes
			is_ai = no
			nationalist_monarchists_are_in_power = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Henri d'Orléans"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_current = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_ready_23_henri_orleans_1908 = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_23 = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_cycle_next }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_choice_23 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = FRA
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
			nationalist_monarchists_are_in_power = yes
		}
		clear_variable = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_preference_23
		COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_default_23 = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_supported = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_initialized }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_initialized
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_set_leader = yes
		}
		else = {
			COLDWAR_FRA_1970_LEADERS_refresh = yes
		}
	}
}
